Contents of [Module:Baro/data](/w/Module:Baro/data "Module:Baro/data") indexed by dates, with each table entry containing the items that Baro has offered on a particular visit. `ConsoleBaroVisits` subtable will contain all Baro visits on console while `PCBaroVisits` will contain all Baro visits on PC. `TennoConBaroVisits` will contain all Baro visits on the [TennoCon](/w/TennoCon "TennoCon") relay during TennoCon.

## Contents

* [1 Data](#Data)
* [2 Code](#Code)
